Fires on the token immediately before an indefinite article ("a"/"an") — i.e., verbs/prepositions like "become", "as", "been", "be", "there's" that predict "a/an" next — and is strongly suppressed on the analogous slot when "the" follows ("copy of the", "met with", "named after the").

L31·N5707unmasked layer
Fires weakly-to-moderately on ordinary English function words/determiners inside fluent boilerplate prose (an, and, our, before a predictable noun), and is very strongly *suppressed* by mojibake/encoding-artifact punctuation (âĢĻ, âĢľ) and by "#" introducing alphanumeric codes — i.e. it tracks "plain English token" vs. "garbled/non-standard character" contexts.

L31·N6360unmasked layer
Fires on isolated single-letter word-initial tokens (a space plus one consonant) where a word has been split across tokens — e.g. " p"|itta, " t"|angle, " s"|avy, " h"|ikers, " b"|rowning — and is strongly suppressed when that split-off initial letter is " d" (dinnertime, duality, dainty).
